Conclusion: rPPG可作为TF深伪检测的有效生理模态；不同生成器具有可解释的生理特性差异，这是本文的主要理论贡献；DeepFakesON-Phys在TF数据上严重退化，凸显了专门针对TF设计的必要性。

Abstract: Talking-face (TF) deepfake generation synthesizes photore- alistic facial video from a static source image and an au- dio signal, producing forgeries that current image-based detectors consistently fail to identify. Unlike face-swap ma- nipulation, TF synthesis has no underlying real video from which to inherit physiological characteristics, making re- mote photoplethysmography (rPPG) a uniquely motivated detection modality for this forgery category. We propose a detection framework that extracts per-video rPPG wave- forms via RhythmFormer and trains a suite of lightweight classifiers to distinguish real from synthesized physiologi- cal signals. Evaluated on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++ un- der a strict subject-independent protocol, where test identi- ties are completely separated from training identities, our 1D ResNet achieves an AUC of 0.806 and EER of 27.8%, placing it within 2.4 points of the best published general- purpose detector (Effort, ICML 2025) while operating ex- clusively on the physiological channel. We document a con- trolled reproduction study of DeepFakesON-Phys, the rep- resentative prior rPPG detector, demonstrating degrada- tion from AUC 0.999 on legacy face-swap data to 0.622 on the TF subset of Celeb-DF++. We further show that detec- tion difficulty is strongly method-dependent: AUC ranges from 0.985 (Real3DPortrait) to 0.690 (IP-LAP) across the seven TF generators, with the ranking remaining perfectly stable across all evaluation protocols. This spread reflects an interpretable physiological property of each generator rather than evaluation noise, and constitutes the primary theoretical contribution of the work.

</details>


### [25] [Bounding the Causal Impact of ML-assisted Decision-Making via Counterfactual Correctness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21806)
*Jonathan Zhang,Erik Skalnes,Jacob Chen,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种部分识别方法，利用先前随机对照试验（RCT）数据为更新后的机器学习模型构建因果效应的界限，通过两个单调性假设（个体层面的反事实正确性和子群预测性能与结果的关系）来约束新模型的因果效应，模拟研究表明该方法能比现有工作提供更紧的界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型在高风险领域（如医疗、刑事司法）的部署日益增多，需要评估其对下游结果的因果影响。RCT能提供高质量证据，但当模型频繁更新或重新训练以提高预测性能时，反复进行RCT往往不可行。因此需要一种方法，能利用先前的RCT数据对新模型的因果效应进行推断。

Method: 提出一种部分识别（partial-identification）方法，利用先前的RCT数据为新模型构建因果效应的界限。核心创新在于利用两个单调性假设将细粒度预测准确性与下游结果联系起来：(1) 个体层面的反事实正确性假设——在其他条件相同的情况下，正确预测导致非劣结果；(2) 子群预测性能与结果之间的单调关系假设——可解释为对模型输出信任程度的假设。

Result: 通过模拟研究证明了该方法的有效性，表明纳入细粒度预测准确性的单调性信息可以比先前工作提供更紧（更有信息量）的因果效应界限。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在无法重新进行RCT的情况下，利用已有RCT数据为新模型提供因果效应的有信息量的界限，为持续更新的ML系统在部署前的因果评估提供了一种可行途径。

Abstract: Predictive machine learning (ML) models are increasingly used to aid human decision-makers across various high-risk domains such as healthcare and criminal justice. There is a growing recognition of the need to evaluate the causal impact of deploying these systems on downstream outcomes, such as patient survival or crime recidivism. Randomized control trials (RCTs) can provide high-quality evidence on the impact of a deployed model, but they run into a challenge: it is often infeasible to run repeated trials when models are updated or retrained to improve predictive performance. In this work, we present a partial-identification approach to using prior RCT data to construct bounds on the causal effect of a new model. The core innovation in our approach is to leverage assumptions relating fine-grained predictive accuracy to downstream outcomes. We do so via two monotonicity assumptions: first, on individual-level `counterfactual correctness' (all else being equal, a correct prediction leads to non-inferior outcomes); and second, on the relation between subgroup predictive performance and outcomes, interpretable as an assumption regarding trust in model outputs. We demonstrate our method with a simulation study, illustrating how incorporating this information can lead to more informative bounds compared to prior work.

</details>


### [26] [Data eccentricity, asymptotics of Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space, and kernel PCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21823)
*Sergio A. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在极大带宽极限下，高斯RBF再生核希尔伯特空间（RKHS）在等向缩放意义下渐近等距于欧氏空间。这一发现表明，依赖RKHS度量性质的核方法（如高斯CKA、核PCA）在大带宽条件下会趋近于线性核的结果。理论和实验均验证了，数据集几何偏心率ρ（最大与中位成对距离之比）可作为预测收敛行为的可靠指标，且RKHS特征嵌入与PCA正交特征框架之间相差一个几何相似变换，残差为O((ρ/σ)²)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示高斯RBF核在大带宽极限下与线性核之间的渐近等价关系，深入理解基于核的方法（如CKA、核PCA）在大带宽条件下的行为，并找到预测数据相关收敛行为的简单指标。

Method: 理论分析：证明高斯RBF核RKHS在σ→∞时等距于欧氏空间；推导CKA和核PCA的渐近行为；建立几何偏心率ρ=最大/中位成对距离作为收敛预测指标。实验验证：在多种数据集上验证ρ对主方向收敛行为的预测能力。

Result: 核PCA的特征值、特征投影和主成分均收敛到经典线性PCA的结果；两种核的特征嵌入和正交PCA特征框架相差一个几何相似变换，残差为O((ρ/σ)²)；ρ能可靠预测数据集的收敛行为。

Conclusion: 高斯RBF核在大带宽极限下渐近等价于线性核，ρ是预测收敛速度的有效指标，为理解核方法的行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We show that, up to isotropic scaling, the Gaussian RBF reproducing kernel Hilbert space (RKHS) is asymptotically isometric to Euclidean space in the large bandwidth limit. This strongly suggests that kernel-based constructions reliant on metric properties of the RKHS will yield results for Gaussian RBF kernels that similarly approach those of linear kernels for large bandwidths. The asymptotic behavior of Gaussian CKA can be understood in this light. We further consider kernel PCA, showing that Gaussian RBF eigenvalues, eigenprojections, and principal components all converge to those of classical (linear) PCA as bandwidth $σ\rightarrow \infty$. For a given data representation, both the RKHS feature embeddings and the orthogonal PCA eigenframes of the two kernel types differ asymptotically by a geometric similarity transformation, up to a residual of size $O \left (\fracρσ \right )^2$, where $ρ$ is a measure of geometric eccentricity of the representation, equal to the ratio of maximum to median pairwise distance between data examples. Experiments over a diverse collection of data sets demonstrate that $ρ$ provides a simple and reliable predictor of dataset-specific convergence behavior in the top principal directions.

</details>


### [27] [A Graph-Based Control Interface for Traffic Signals on Heterogeneous Road Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21831)
*Bertil Braun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于共享图神经网络的交通信号控制接口，使用确定性关联矩阵将运动评分转换为可变大小的信号相位集合。通过PPO实验在多种未见过的图结构上评估，结果表明在合成网格族内性能保持，但对信号覆盖分布变化敏感，单个城市策略可在多个异质城市图上执行但结果异质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通信号控制方法中，图大小和路口特定动作数量与学习网络参数形状耦合，限制了模型向不同拓扑结构路网的泛化能力。本文旨在解耦这种依赖关系，使策略能适应可变大小的图结构和路口动作空间。

Method: 设计共享图神经网络接口：1) 使用有向走廊节点提供交通上下文，运动节点表示受控的输入到输出路径；2) 类型均值聚合为每个运动生成标量评分；3) 通过确定性关联矩阵将评分转换为可变大小的合法信号相位集合；4) 相位定义和信号时序保持在网络外部，使图大小和路口动作数与学习参数形状解耦；5) 使用PPO进行训练。

Result: 1) 在未见过的合成网格几何结构上保持性能；2) 信号覆盖变化暴露了对分布偏移的敏感性；3) 单个城市策略可在5个异质城市图上执行，但结果呈现异质性。

Conclusion: 该接口提供了可行性证据，表明图神经网络可解耦参数形状与图结构实现跨拓扑泛化，但不能作为向任意路网迁移的一般性估计。信号覆盖分布偏移是当前方法的主要限制。

Abstract: We present a traffic-signal control interface in which a shared graph neural network assigns scores to individual traffic movements. Each junction converts these scores into its own variable-sized set of legal signal phases using a deterministic incidence matrix. Directed corridor nodes provide traffic context, while movement nodes represent controlled input-to-output paths through junctions. Typed mean aggregation produces one scalar per movement; phase definitions and signal timing remain outside the learned network. This makes graph size and junction-specific action count independent of the learned parameter shapes. PPO experiments evaluate the interface on unseen synthetic grid geometries, altered signal coverage, and five heterogeneous city graphs. The policies retained performance across unseen geometries within the synthetic grid family, while changes in signal coverage exposed sensitivity to a signal-coverage distribution shift. A single trained city-policy instance executed across all five city graphs, with heterogeneous outcomes. These results provide feasibility evidence rather than a general estimate of transfer to arbitrary road networks.

</details>


### [28] [Searching the Space of Feed-Forward Neural-Network Weight-Update Rules with Fixed Depth Symbolic Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21855)
*Charles Brum,Edward Finkelstein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索了符号回归是否能够自动发现比人工设计的标准优化器更优的神经网络权重更新规则。在30个基准/网络组合中，符号回归在25个案例中找到了超越最优调参标准优化器的更新规则，MSE平均降低44.47%。发现的规则形式多样，但多结合了自适应归一化、动量项、非线性变换和有理表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的神经网络优化器大多由人工设计，本文希望验证是否可以通过符号回归自动发现性能更优的、紧凑的权重更新规则，从而减少对人工调参和手工设计优化器的依赖。

Method: 将候选更新规则表示为基于常见优化器操作数（梯度、动量、自适应梯度、矩估计等）的固定深度符号表达式，在小型符号回归基准上对神经网络进行符号回归搜索，并与超参数调优后的标准优化器进行对比。

Result: 在30个基准/网络组合中，25个案例下符号回归发现了优于最佳调参标准优化器的更新规则；在改进的案例中，MSE累计降低44.47%。发现的规则没有统一的符号形式，但普遍包含自适应归一化、动量项、非线性变换和有理表达式等元素。

Conclusion: 符号回归可以作为发现紧凑优化器变体的轻量级方法，但需要更大规模的验证来进一步确认其泛化能力和实用性。

Abstract: We investigate whether symbolic regression can discover explicit neural network weight-update rules that outperform standard hand-designed optimizers on small symbolic regression benchmarks. Candidate update rules are represented as fixed-depth symbolic expressions over operands derived from common optimizers, including gradient, momentum, adaptive-gradient, and moment-estimate quantities. Across 30 benchmark/neural network combinations, the symbolic regression procedure found an update rule outperforming the best hyperparameter-tuned established optimizer in 25 cases, with an aggregate MSE reduction of 44.47\% over the improved cases. The discovered rules do not all share a single common symbolic form, but many combine adaptive normalization, momentum-like quantities, nonlinear transformations, and rational expressions. These results suggest that symbolic regression can serve as a lightweight mechanism for discovering compact optimizer variants, while also highlighting the need for larger-scale validation.

</details>


### [29] [LeAct: Learning to Reason from Expert Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21856)
*Ziran Yang,Chengshuai Shi,Raj Ghugare,Benjamin Eysenbach,Karthik Narasimhan,Chi Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LeAct框架，通过将专家系统的动作视为潜在变量，逆向恢复其背后的推理链（CoT），从而将无声专家系统转化为推理教师，训练出泛化能力更强的学生模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家系统（如游戏引擎、经典规划器、定理证明器）能产生接近最优的动作，但缺乏自然语言推理过程的记录。如何从这些"沉默专家"的动作中恢复出可解释的思维链，以蒸馏其知识到学生模型中。

Method: 将专家动作背后的思维链视为潜在变量，采用LeAct框架进行优化：学生模型为每个专家动作采样候选CoT，只保留那些能显著提升学生自身生成该动作概率的CoT，从而迭代优化潜在变量。

Result: 在不完美信息博弈（多个规模）和模拟机器人基准测试上：小规模可枚举游戏达到求解器数值下限；大规模场景下比最强专家迭代基线优5倍；在Flop Hold'em（约10^9信息集）上胜率+60 mbb/g；机器人任务中是唯一优于直接模仿的训练方法。

Conclusion: 专家系统可以成为基础模型的一种全新推理教师来源，LeAct提供了一种从无声专家的动作中恢复推理过程的原理性框架。

Abstract: Modern reasoning models depend on reasoning data, today sourced from human annotations or distilled from stronger LLMs. However, a rich and largely untapped source of supervision lies in expert systems (e.g., game engines, classical planners, theorem provers), which routinely produce near-optimal actions across diverse domains. But these experts are silent: they commit to an action without writing down the chain of thought (CoT) behind it. Recovering that CoT as natural-language reasoning would distill expert knowledge into a student that generalizes beyond the demonstrated actions. We treat it as a latent variable and study how to recover it from the action alone. Our approach, LeAct (Learning to reason from Actions), optimizes this latent variable: the student samples candidate CoTs for each expert action, and we retain those that measurably improve its own probability of recovering the action. Across imperfect-information games at multiple scales and a simulated robotics benchmark, LeAct reaches the solver's numerical floor on small enumerable games. At larger scale, it is $5\times$ closer to the solver than the strongest expert-iteration baseline. At Flop Hold'em ($\sim 10^9$ infosets), LeAct wins head-to-head by $+60$ mbb/g, and on the robotics probe it is the only training recipe that improves on direct imitation. We present a principled framework and the result: expert systems become a categorically new source of reasoning teachers for foundation models.

</details>


### [30] [Scaling Laws for Classical Machine Learning on Tabular Data: A Benchmark Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21866)
*Kaihua Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过127名学生的大规模课堂复现实验，在18个表格数据集和6种模型家族上进行了11,536次训练，拟合了1,648条幂律学习曲线。主要发现：(1)幂律拟合良好（77.7%单元格R²>0.8），树集成模型优于线性模型；(2)同一模型家族内指数近似可共享（5/6个家族），但仅达到近似预测可压缩性，非数据集无关的普适性；(3)固定随机种子后，不同实现间指数仍存在显著差异（CV(b)=0.144），源于协议中未约束的数据预处理环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统经典机器学习的学习曲线研究多限于小规模（单曲线、单团队、少量数据集），且聚焦于树模型、线性模型和核模型在表格数据上的幂律拟合。本文旨在大规模、可复现的课堂复现实验框架下，系统验证幂律学习曲线在多种模型家族和数据集上的适用性及局限性。

Method: 采用分布式课堂复现范式：127名学生各自对3个指定数据集（共18个表格分类/回归数据集）运行固定协议，涵盖6种模型家族（Boosting、Random Forest、SVM、Linear/Logistic、Ridge、Lasso），累计11,536次训练运行。对每条学习曲线拟合幂律形式error(N)=aN^(-b)+c，共得到1,648条拟合曲线。通过R²评估拟合质量，通过跨数据集共享指数分析和AIC比较评估指数可压缩性，通过固定随机种子下不同实现间的指数变异评估复现方差。

Result: (1)幂律拟合效果良好：77.7%的单元格R²>0.8；全数据量下树集成模型占优（Boosting在50%数据集上最优，Random Forest在33%上最优），线性模型在分类任务上表现不佳。(2)模型家族内指数近似可共享：5/6个家族中，单一家族级指数预测跨数据集曲线的R²差距<0.011；但AIC偏好非约束拟合，曲线塌缩部分（32-58%的点在±0.5 dex内）。Lasso完全失败（阴性对照），Ridge在留一数据集检验下脆弱。(3)固定random_state=42后，不同实现间指数变异均值CV(b)=0.144，源于协议中未约束的预处理、编码、缺失值处理等环节。

Conclusion: 幂律学习曲线在大规模多模型多数据集场景下仍具良好拟合能力，但模型家族内指数共享仅达到"近似预测可压缩性"而非真正的"数据集无关普适性"；同时，学习曲线拟合结果对协议中未明确约束的预处理选择敏感，凸显了机器学习研究中可复现性挑战。本文发布了聚合曲线、单元格拟合结果以及达到目标误差0.15所需数据量的实用参考表。

Abstract: Prior classical-ML learning-curve work fits power laws to tree, linear, and kernel models on tabular data, but at small scale: typically one curve, one team, a handful of cells. We present a distributed classroom-scale replication: 127 students each ran a fixed protocol on 3 assigned datasets, drawn from 18 tabular classification and regression datasets and 6 model families (Boosting, Random Forest, SVM, Linear/Logistic, Ridge, Lasso), yielding 11,536 training runs and 1,648 fitted power-law curves of the form error(N) = a N^(-b) + c. Three findings. (1) Power laws fit: R^2 > 0.8 on 77.7% of cells, with tree ensembles dominating at full data (Boosting 50% of datasets, RandomForest 33%; linear models underperform on classification). (2) Approximate shared exponents within a model family: for 5 of 6 families, a single family-level exponent predicts each family's cross-dataset curves nearly as well as per-dataset exponents (R^2 gap < 0.011), though AIC favors the unconstrained fit and curve collapse is partial (32-58% of points within +/-0.5 dex). We frame this as approximate predictive compressibility, not dataset-independent universality; Lasso fails outright (negative control) and Ridge is fragile under leave-one-dataset-out. (3) Replicator-implementation variance: with random_state=42 fixed, independent re-implementations of the same protocol still differ by mean CV(b) = 0.144 on the fitted exponent -- not seed variance, but the spread induced by unconstrained parts of the protocol (preprocessing, encoding, missing-value handling). We release the aggregated curves, per-cell fits, and a practical data-requirement table for N* to reach target error 0.15.

</details>


### [31] [Variance-Reduced Q-Learning over Static and Time-Varying Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21876)
*Sreejeet Maity,Feng Zhu,Aritra Mitra,Robert W. Heath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为VRDQ的分布式Q-learning算法，在多智能体共享同一MDP的场景下，通过基于epoch的架构和共识协议实现协作学习。该算法在静态和时变网络上均能达到线性加速的收敛速度，且仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$的通信成本，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决多智能体分布式强化学习中通信效率与样本效率之间的权衡问题，现有方法虽然能实现样本复杂度的线性加速，但通信成本过高。

Method: 提出VRDQ算法：采用epoch-based分布式Q-learning框架，每个epoch内智能体本地估计Bellman最优性算子，并通过基于共识的协议在网络中扩散信息。

Result: 在静态和时变网络上，VRDQ均实现了高概率有限时间收敛，且收敛速度具有线性加速效果；样本复杂度上的线性加速仅需$\tilde{O}(1)$通信量。

Conclusion: VRDQ在保持样本效率线性加速的同时，大幅降低了通信开销，为分布式强化学习的实际部署提供了更高效的方案。

Abstract: We investigate a decentralized reinforcement learning problem involving multiple agents that interact with the same Markov Decision Process (MDP). The agents can exchange information over a network to collectively learn the optimal state-action value function. For this setting, we introduce a novel epoch-based distributed $Q$-learning algorithm called VRDQ, where within each epoch, agents locally estimate the Bellman optimality operator and diffuse information using a consensus-based protocol. For both static and time-varying networks, we establish high-probability finite-time convergence rates for VRDQ that enjoy linear speedups from collaboration. Crucially, we prove that such speedups in sample-complexity require only $\tilde{O}(1)$ communication, substantially improving upon the communication costs in prior work.

</details>


### [32] [Remedying Coarsening-Based GNN Training under Heterophily via Adaptive Complementary Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21885)
*Guoming Li,Jian Yang,Xukun Wang,Zixiao Wang,Shangsong Liang,Yifan Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对图神经网络(GNN)的粗化训练在异配图上性能严重下降的问题，本文提出自适应互补增强(ACE)策略，通过重构原始节点特征和各向异性结构正则化来恢复粗化过程中丢失的信息，并采用同质不确定性加权自适应平衡训练目标，在异配基准上取得持续改进，同时保持同配图上的竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有粗化训练方法几乎仅在同配图上评估，在更具挑战性的异配图设置下表现严重下降，但该问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出ACE（自适应互补增强），包含：(1) 学习投影器重构原始节点特征；(2) 各向异性结构正则化嵌入局部异配性；(3) 同质不确定性加权自适应平衡粗化图训练损失与全图辅助损失。

Result: 在异配图基准上取得持续性能提升，同时在同配图上保持有竞争力的结果，且计算开销极小。

Conclusion: ACE作为一种即插即用、模型无关的策略，有效解决了粗化训练在异配图上的信息丢失问题，拓展了粗化训练方法的适用范围。

Abstract: Coarsening-based training for graph neural networks (GNNs), i.e.\ training on coarsened graphs rather than the original large ones, has become a promising direction for scaling GNNs to massive graphs. However, prior work has been evaluated almost exclusively on \textit{homophilic} graphs, leaving the more challenging \textit{heterophilic} settings underexplored. We show, both empirically and theoretically, that existing coarsening-based training methods suffer significant performance degradation on heterophilic graphs due to inevitable loss of graph information during coarsening. To address this, we propose {\bf A}daptive {\bf C}omplementary {\bf E}nhancement, a plug-and-play, model-agnostic strategy that reintegrates the information discarded in coarsening: ACE learns a projector for re-constructing original node features and applies \textit{anisotropic structural regularization} to embed local heterophily. We further adopt \textit{homoscedastic uncertainty weighting} to adaptively balance the combined training objective of primary coarsened-graph training loss and full-graph auxiliary loss with augmented node features re-constructed by the heterophily-aware projector. Extensive experiments show that ACE drives consistent gains on heterophilic benchmarks while preserving competitive results on homophilic graphs with minimal computational overhead. Code is available at the GitHub repository: https://github.com/vasile-paskardlgm/ACE.

</details>


### [33] [MissHyper: Restoring Clinical Synchronicity in Missingness-Guided Hypergraph Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21922)
*Mingyi Ma,Qingxiong Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MissHyper模型，通过缺失感知的超图预测框架，在消息传递前恢复共时间戳上下文（快照恢复），解决临床不规则多变量时间序列预测中事件初始化信息瓶颈问题，在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV数据集的几步预测中持续取得改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件中心模型过早展平共时间戳结构，同一时间戳采集的测量值被嵌入为孤立节点，导致局部患者状态上下文在后续消息传递层之前不可用，形成预传播表示瓶颈。

Method: MissHyper包含三个核心组件：1) 支持密度编码，为每个事件增强局部密度线索；2) 快照恢复，聚合共时间戳记录以恢复患者状态上下文；3) 缺失感知门控，自适应融合节点特异性证据与恢复的上下文信息。

Result: 在PhysioNet 2012、MIMIC-III和MIMIC-IV三个临床数据集上，MissHyper在多步预测任务中持续优于强超图基线，消融实验表明快照恢复、自适应融合和支持密度编码均有贡献。

Conclusion: 改进事件初始化能够在不重新设计下游传播架构的情况下提升稀疏临床预测性能，事件初始化是稀疏临床预测中一个关键的设计维度。

Abstract: Clinical irregular multivariate time series are shaped not only by physiological dynamics but also by the measurement process that determines when and what to observe. In event-centric models, however, co-timestamp structure can be flattened too early: measurements acquired at the same timestamp are embedded as isolated nodes, leaving local patient-state context unavailable until later message-passing layers. We study this pre-propagation representation bottleneck and address it by restoring co-timestamp context before message passing begins. We propose MissHyper, a missingness-guided hypergraph forecasting model with pre-propagation synchronicity restoration. MissHyper augments each event with a local support-density cue, aggregates co-timestamp records to recover patient-state context, and uses a missingness-guided gate to adaptively fuse node-specific evidence with the recovered context. Across PhysioNet 2012, MIMIC-III, and MIMIC-IV, MissHyper achieves consistent gains in multi-step forecasting and outperforms a strong hypergraph baseline. These results suggest that improving event initialization can benefit sparse clinical forecasting without requiring a redesigned downstream propagation architecture. Ablations indicate that snapshot restoration, adaptive fusion, and support-density encoding all contribute, pointing to event initialization as a critical design axis for sparse clinical forecasting.

</details>


### [34] [RIS-Kernel: A Model-Agnostic Architecture for Long-Context LLM Inference via Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21927)
*Anderson R. Santos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS)推理引擎，通过稀疏随机几何方法将自注意力复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N log N)，使得长上下文LLM推理可在无GPU的标准硬件上运行。在Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct上验证，1%密度+70个种子的RIS-Stochastic在32,768 token上达到75.00%准确率，超越原生密集注意力的71.88%；在65,536 token上，RIS在密集注意力OOM的情况下取得了最高14.06个百分点的检索增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的全自注意力机制计算复杂度为O(N²)，限制了长上下文文档分析到65,536个token，且需要昂贵的GPU集群。现有方法在内存受限的硬件上难以实现高效的长上下文推理。

Method: 提出RIS推理引擎，是一种模型无关的架构，无需修改权重。通过稀疏随机几何方法降低自注意力复杂度至O(N log N)，使其适配商品内存限制。RIS包含两种变体：RIS-Stochastic（随机采样）和RIS-Structural（结构采样），通过控制密度和集成种子数量调节稀疏度。

Result: 在Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct上，32,768 token时：RIS-Stochastic（1%密度，70种子）达75.00%准确率，超越密集基线71.88%；RIS-Stochastic（5%密度，10种子）匹配基线71.88%；RIS-Structural（1%密度，10种子）达68.75%，恢复75%上下文差距。65,536 token时，密集注意力OOM，RIS取得最高14.06个百分点的检索增益（McNemar检验p=0.078）。所有实验在16-128 GB RAM的无GPU标准CPU服务器上运行。

Conclusion: 稀疏注意力可作为正则化器：低密度（1%）多种子能过滤序列级噪声，高密度（5%）会重新引入干扰噪声。RIS证明了无需GPU加速，在标准学术硬件上进行长上下文LLM推理是可行的，为长文档分析提供了实用且经济的解决方案。

Abstract: Full self-attention in large language models scales as O(N^2), which limits long-context document analysis to 65,536 tokens and requires costly GPU clusters. The Reduced Interaction Sampling (RIS) inference engine addresses this constraint as a model-agnostic architecture. Without modifying weights, RIS reduces self-attention complexity to O(N log N) using sparse stochastic geometry that fits within commodity memory limits. We validate RIS on Qwen2-1.5B-Instruct across two regimes. In controlled evaluations at 32,768 tokens (where native dense attention serves as the upper bound), RIS-Stochastic at 1% density and 70 ensemble seeds achieves 75.00% accuracy, outperforming the native dense baseline (71.88%), while RIS-Stochastic at 5% density and 10 seeds matches it (71.88%). This demonstrates that sparse attention acts as a regularizer: low density (1%) over multiple seeds filters out sequence-level noise, whereas higher density (5%) reintroduces distractor noise. Under the tightest budget, RIS-Structural reaches 68.75% accuracy at 1% density with just 10 seeds, recovering 75% of the contextual gap relative to the zero-context floor (59.38%). At 65,536 tokens, where dense attention triggers out-of-memory faults, RIS yields retrieval gains of up to 14.06 percentage points over the zero-context floor (51.56%), which is confirmed as marginally significant under McNemar's paired test (p = 0.078 < 0.10). All evaluations run on commodity, unaccelerated CPU servers (16-128 GB of RAM), demonstrating that long-context LLM inference is feasible on standard academic hardware without GPU acceleration.

</details>


### [35] [LatentFlow: Visual Analytics for Latent Space Analysis in Molecular Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21941)
*Shiyi Liu,Jiaqing Chen,Nicholas Hadler,Rostyslav Hnatyshyn,Michael W. Mahoney,Talita Perciano,John F. Hartwig,Gunther H. Weber,Ross Maciejewski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentFlow是一个可视化分析系统，用于分析分子图神经网络中潜在空间的演化，通过改进的Sankey图追踪聚类跨层和跨模型状态的变化，帮助科学家理解模型行为并识别有意义的化学模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法对跨层和跨不同模型状态（如训练轮次、模型配置、输入数据）的潜在空间分析支持有限，难以理解潜在空间如何在模型中演化以及与化学概念的关系。

Method: 提出LatentFlow可视化分析系统：将嵌入向量聚类，使用改进的Sankey图追踪聚类跨层和跨模型状态的变化，链接聚类到代表性分子及其共享子结构，支持科学家引入领域知识与潜在空间模式进行比较。

Result: 通过两个案例研究评估，结果表明LatentFlow帮助科学家理解潜在空间如何演化、识别有意义的分子模式，并更好地解释模型行为。

Conclusion: LatentFlow有效解决了分子GNN潜在空间跨层、跨状态分析困难的问题，为理解模型内部表征与化学概念的关系提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Chemists and materials scientists increasingly use machine learning models, such as graph neural networks (GNNs), to predict properties of molecules and the outcomes of their reactions. Beyond predictive performance, understanding how these models organize chemical information internally in their latent spaces, i.e., the embeddings of the molecules, is critical. Analyzing latent spaces helps diagnose model behavior and assess whether the learned embeddings are organized in ways that reflect meaningful chemical relationships. Unfortunately, existing methods provide limited support for analyzing latent spaces across layers and across different model states (e.g., training epochs, model configurations, and input data), making it difficult to understand how these latent spaces evolve throughout a model or relate to chemical concepts. We present LatentFlow, a visual analytics system developed in collaboration with a domain expert for analyzing latent spaces in molecular GNNs. LatentFlow groups embeddings into clusters and supports exploration of latent spaces by tracking how these clusters change across layers and model states using a modified Sankey diagram. To support interpretation, LatentFlow links these clusters to representative molecules and their shared substructures, and it allows scientists to introduce their own domain knowledge and compare it with the patterns found in the latent spaces. We evaluate LatentFlow through two case studies. The results show that LatentFlow helps scientists understand how latent spaces evolve, identify meaningful molecular patterns, and better interpret model behavior.

</details>


### [36] [Multi-Agent Debate and Visual Information Extraction for SeePhys Pro: A 1st-Place Technical Report from ICML 2026 AI4Math Track 3 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21946)
*Jiseok Kwak,Suhyeon Jo,Taewoo Kim,Yeongmin Kim,Byeonghu Na,Il-chul Moon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段框架来解决大学物理视觉问题：第一阶段通过视觉信息提取将图像内容转化为文本以弥合模态差距，第二阶段通过多智能体辩论协调三个异构求解器。分析表明，性能提升主要来自可靠的答案选择而非额外辩论，且图像辅助的价值与问题中图像包含的信息量成正比。该管道在公开集上将准确率从0.643提升至0.802，并在公开和私有排行榜上均获得第一名（私有集整体0.743）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理视觉物理问题时存在显著的模态差距，当关键信息位于图像而非文本中时，模型性能大幅下降，且这种差距会随着更多问题内容迁移到图像中而加剧。现有方法缺乏有效弥合这一差距的机制。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）视觉信息提取阶段，将图像中的内容重新表达为求解器可读的文本，以弥合模态差距；2）推理阶段，通过多智能体辩论协调三个异构求解器进行答案选择和生成。

Result: 在公开集上，整体准确率从单智能体基线的0.643提升至0.802；在私有集上整体准确率达0.743，获得公开和私有排行榜双料第一名。

Conclusion: 性能提升主要源于可靠的答案选择机制而非额外的辩论过程；图像辅助的价值与问题中图像所包含的信息量成正比。

Abstract: This technical report presents our approach to Challenge Track~3: SeePhys Pro at the 3rd AI for Math Workshop, where the task is to answer college-level physics questions whose statement and figure may be given partly or entirely as an image. Visual physics problems become substantially harder for large language models when the decisive information resides in a figure rather than in the text, and this modality gap widens as more of the problem migrates into the image. We address the task with a two-stage framework: a visual information extraction stage that re-expresses figure content as solver-readable text to close the modality gap, and a reasoning stage that orchestrates three heterogeneous solvers through multi-agent debate. Our analysis yields two findings: the gain from orchestration comes from reliable answer selection rather than from additional debate, and the value of a figure aid scales with how much of the problem is locked inside the image. The resulting pipeline improves overall accuracy over a single-agent baseline from 0.643 to 0.802 on the public split, and won 1st place on both the public and the private leaderboard (private overall 0.743).

</details>


### [37] [MA-DAR: Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing for Continual Temporal Knowledge Graph Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21949)
*Xiangjun Shi,Chong Mu,Jinchuan Zhang,Lizong Zhang,Yuefeng He,Shang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对持续时序知识图谱推理中的表征冲突问题（范数主导与语义模糊），提出MA-DAR轻量级框架，通过流形对齐、动态自适应路由和极化正则化实现回放表征与当前表征的深度融合，显著提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于回放的持续学习方法主要关注"回放什么"（what to replay），却忽视了"回放表征如何与当前表征融合"（how to integrate）。直接融合导致范数主导和语义模糊两种表征冲突，降低持续推理性能。

Method: 提出MA-DAR三元组设计：(1)流形对齐：将回放表征和当前表征对齐到共享流形以缓解分布差异；(2)动态门控机制：学习维度级融合权重，自适应确定两者贡献度；(3)极化正则化：惩罚模棱两可的门控决策，促使更果断的路由行为。

Result: 在四个公开持续TKG基准数据集上，MA-DAR作为轻量级即插即用框架，一致提升了多种代表性TKG编码器的性能，在不同回放设置下均保持有效性。消融实验和可视化分析验证了各模块在缓解表征冲突中的作用。

Conclusion: MA-DAR通过解决表征融合中的范数主导和语义模糊问题，有效提升了持续时序知识图谱推理的性能，为回放式持续学习提供了更优的融合范式。

Abstract: Continual temporal knowledge graph (TKG) reasoning aims to continuously incorporate newly emerging facts while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Replay-based continual learning has achieved promising performance by revisiting historical representations. However, existing methods primarily focus on what to replay, while largely overlooking how replayed representations should be integrated with current ones. Such direct integration often gives rise to two critical forms of representation conflict: \textit{norm domination} and \textit{semantic blurring}, ultimately degrading continual reasoning performance. To address these challenges, we propose MA-DAR (Manifold-Aligned Dynamic Adaptive Routing), a lightweight plug-and-play framework for replay representation fusion. MA-DAR first aligns replayed and current representations onto a shared manifold to alleviate distribution discrepancies. It then employs a dynamic gating mechanism to learn dimension-wise fusion weights, adaptively determining the contribution of replayed and current representations to the fused representation. Furthermore, a polarization regularizer encourages more decisive routing behaviors by discouraging ambiguous gating decisions, resulting in more stable and effective knowledge integration. Extensive experiments on four public continual TKG benchmarks demonstrate that MA-DAR consistently improves the performance of representative TKG encoders while remaining effective under different replay settings. Comprehensive ablation studies and visualization analyses further verify the effectiveness of manifold alignment and dynamic adaptive routing in mitigating representation conflicts and improving continual reasoning.

</details>


### [38] [Teaching LLMs to Self-Evolve: Cultivating Core Meta-Skills with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21971)
*Shujin Wu,Cheng Qian,Xiusi Chen,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaEvolve框架，通过数据合成、进化感知强化学习和推理时进化搜索，显式培养模型自我进化的元技能（如自我反思与环境反馈利用），在代码生成任务上显著超越基线，并展现出跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有测试时缩放方法（如AlphaEvolve）依赖迭代自我进化与环境反馈，其成功关键在于元技能（自我反思与环境反馈利用），但传统后训练方法忽视了这些元技能。

Method: 基于代码执行提供连续奖励信号（正确性+效率），合成进化轨迹作为训练数据，每条轨迹包含当前程序、适应度分数和历史尝试；通过可验证奖励的强化学习在大规模代码数据上训练模型，培养跨领域通用的元技能。

Result: 在7个编码基准上，分布内任务比最强基线绝对提升10.01%，分布外任务提升24.12%；在完全域外的开放算法优化问题上，相对提升46.9%。

Conclusion: 显式培养自我进化元技能是实现更强大、能自动自我进化AI的原则性路径。

Abstract: Test-time scaling through iterative self-evolution with environment feedback, as demonstrated by AlphaEvolve, shows remarkable performance gains. We hypothesize that the success of such evolution frameworks hinges on meta-skills, such as self-reflection with environment feedback, that enable effective multi-round refinement, yet are largely neglected by traditional post-training. To bridge this gap, we present MetaEvolve, a framework designed to develop these meta-skills via a data synthesis pipeline, evolution-aware reinforcement learning (RL), and inference-time evolutionary search. Concretely, we ground MetaEvolve in coding, where program execution provides natural, continuous reward signals beyond binary correctness. Building on these signals, we synthesize evolution trajectories as training data, each containing a current program, its fitness score (combining correctness and efficiency), and a history of prior attempts, and train the model via RL with verifiable rewards derived from test case execution. By training on large-scale code data, we aim to inspire generalizable domain-agnostic meta-skills that can transfer broadly to open-ended problems where such rich training signals are scarce. Across seven coding benchmarks, MetaEvolve outperforms the strongest baseline by 10.01% absolute on in-distribution tasks and 24.12% on out-of-distribution tasks. On open-ended algorithm optimization problems entirely outside the training domain, it further achieves a 46.9% relative improvement. These results demonstrate that explicitly cultivating self-evolution meta-skills offers a principled path toward more capable and autonomously self-evolving AI.

</details>


### [39] [On the Convergence of Stochastic Low-Rank Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21975)
*Ru Wang,Chengchang Liu,John C. S. Lui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对低秩适配（LoRA）的优化复杂度进行理论分析，将确定性场景下LoRA-GD达到ε-驻点的全梯度计算复杂度从exp{O(ε^{-2})}改进为O(ε^{-4})；在随机梯度场景下提出LoRA-NSGDM（复杂度O(ε^{-8})）和LoRA-STORM（利用方差缩减，复杂度O(ε^{-6})）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA优化理论分析给出的确定性梯度计算复杂度为exp{O(ε^{-2})}，过于宽松且不具实用性；同时随机场景下的LoRA优化缺乏严格的理论保证。本文旨在收紧确定性LoRA的复杂度上界，并建立随机LoRA优化的理论框架。

Method: 在确定性场景下通过更精细的分析将LoRA-GD的复杂度降至O(ε^{-4})；在随机场景下提出LoRA-NSGDM（基于非凸随机梯度下降）实现O(ε^{-8})复杂度，并进一步引入方差缩减方法LoRA-STORM，在均方光滑条件下将复杂度降至O(ε^{-6})。

Result: 确定性LoRA-GD：O(ε^{-4})全梯度评估；随机LoRA-NSGDM：O(ε^{-8})随机梯度复杂度；随机LoRA-STORM（方差缩减）：O(ε^{-6})随机梯度复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文显著改进了LoRA优化的理论复杂度界，并为随机LoRA提供了首个严格的理论保证，方差缩减策略能有效提升随机LoRA的收敛效率。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) optimizes $J(B,A)=\mathcal L(W_\mathrm{base}+sBA)$ over two adapters $B \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times r}$ and $A \in \mathbb{R}^{r \times n}$ that form a low-rank update to a frozen pretrained weight matrix $W_\mathrm{base} \in \mathbb{R}^{m \times n}$. The prior analysis shows LoRA-GD takes $\exp\{\mathcal{O}(ε^{-2})\}$ oracle calls to find an $ε$-stationary point such that $\|\nabla J(B,A)\|\leq ε$ in the deterministic setting. We sharpen the analysis and show that $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-4})$ full-gradient evaluations suffice for the same first-order criterion. We further study stochastic LoRA under unbiased gradient estimates and finite variance. We propose LoRA-NSGDM, which finds an $ε$-stationary point with $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-8})$ stochastic oracle complexity. Under the additional mean-square smoothness condition, we use variance reduction strategy and propose LoRA-STORM, which improves the stochastic oracle complexity to $\mathcal{O}(ε^{-6})$.

</details>


### [40] [From Perturbation Correction to Geometry-Aware Sampling: Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling for Balanced Flat Minima in Long-Tailed Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.21999)
*Jiaxin Deng,Junbiao Pang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling (SGS)，通过动态调整采样分布来同时解决长尾学习中的类别不平衡和锐利极小值问题，仅依赖累积类别计数和EMA锐度估计，无需额外反向传播，在CIFAR-100 LT和ImageNet-LT上显著提升尾类精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾学习中，头部类别主导训练暴露，而尾部类别收敛到损失景观的锐利区域，导致泛化差。传统重采样仅处理前者，不考虑几何特性；现有长尾SAM方法仅在偏置小批量上修改损失或扰动。缺乏一种联合控制数据暴露与优化几何的采样机制。

Method: SGS将采样分布视为优化几何的主动控制变量，动态调整后续小批量：增加低频采样类别的概率，抑制SAM诱导损失变化大的类别。仅使用累积类别计数和从标准SAM更新中获得的EMA锐度估计，无需类别级扰动或额外反向传播。通过连续时间随机微分方程和采样依赖的PAC-Bayes分析刻画采样过程。

Result: 在CIFAR-100 LT（不平衡比100）上，SGS-SAM相比Focal-SAM，尾类精度提升10.85个百分点，总体提升3.56个百分点。在ImageNet-LT上，相比ImbSAM，尾类提升6.59个百分点，总体提升1.20个百分点。训练时间仅为vanilla SAM的1.02倍。

Conclusion: SGS开辟了一条通过采样侧控制损失景观的新路径，表明未来的长尾方法可以联合调节数据暴露和优化几何，而非将二者视为固定因素。

Abstract: Long-tailed learning couples two sources of poor generalization: head classes dominate training exposure, while under-represented classes often converge to sharper regions of the loss landscape. Conventional re-sampling addresses the former without considering geometry, whereas existing long-tailed sharpness-aware minimization (SAM) methods modify losses or perturbations only after biased mini-batches have been drawn. We introduce Sharpness-Guided Equilibrium Sampling (SGS), which treats the sampling distribution as an active control variable for optimization geometry. SGS dynamically adjusts subsequent mini-batches by increasing the sampling probability of less frequently sampled classes while suppressing classes with large SAM-induced loss changes, using only cumulative class counts and EMA sharpness estimates obtained from the standard SAM update, without class-wise perturbations or additional backward passes. We characterize this sampling process through a continuous-time stochastic differential equation and a sampling-dependent PAC-Bayes analysis, explaining how frequency-sharpness feedback can move training toward a more balanced flatness profile. On CIFAR-100 LT with an imbalance ratio of 100, SGS-SAM improves Focal-SAM by 10.85 points in tail accuracy and 3.56 points overall. On ImageNet-LT, it improves ImbSAM by 6.59 points on tail classes and 1.20 points overall. Its training time is only $1.02\times$ that of vanilla SAM. Beyond these gains, SGS establishes a sampling-side route to loss-landscape control, suggesting that future long-tailed methods can jointly regulate data exposure and optimization geometry rather than treating either as fixed.

</details>


### [41] [Energy Manifold Natural Gradient Descent: Riemannian Optimization for Neural PDE Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22004)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMNGD，一种用于参数位于黎曼流形上的物理信息和变分神经PDE求解器的流形优化框架，通过约束参数更新到可行切方向并使用回缩保持约束，在神经PDE基准上达到更高精度和更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有能量自然梯度下降（ENGD）假设无约束欧几里得参数空间，无法处理参数位于黎曼流形上的物理信息和变分神经PDE求解器的优化问题。

Method: 提出EMNGD框架，将能量诱导的二次模型限制在可行切方向，使用回缩操作保持参数约束；证明其推前方向是函数空间牛顿向量的最佳可行近似；利用Woodbury恒等式将切系统转换到样本空间，并通过Nyström近似提供可扩展的样本空间求解。

Result: 在神经PDE基准上，EMNGD相比现有最先进基线实现了更高精度和更快收敛；Woodbury保持EMNGD方向不变，可扩展求解器诊断量化了预条件和残差子采样的精度-成本权衡。

Conclusion: EMNGD建立了坐标不变性、欧几里得空间中退化为ENGD、全局一阶收敛（Armijo回溯）以及对不精确切求解的鲁棒性，为约束参数空间的能量优化提供了理论保证和实践优势。

Abstract: Energy natural gradient descent (ENGD) aligns parameter updates with the curvature of an underlying function-space energy, but existing formulations assume an unconstrained Euclidean parameter domain. We introduce \EMNGDfull{}, a manifold optimization framework for physics-informed and variational neural PDE solvers whose parameters lie on a Riemannian manifold. EMNGD restricts the energy-induced quadratic model to feasible tangent directions and uses retractions to preserve parameter constraints throughout optimization. Under coercivity, we prove that the push-forward of the undamped EMNGD direction is the best feasible approximation to the function-space Newton vector in the energy metric. We establish coordinate invariance, exact reduction to ENGD in Euclidean space, global first-order convergence with Armijo backtracking, and robustness to inexact tangent solves. For quadratic residual energies and generalized Gauss--Newton pullbacks, the Woodbury identity transfers the tangent system to sample space without changing the direction. Nyström approximation provides scalable sample-space solves with controlled direction error and recovers the exact direction after iterative convergence. On the evaluated neural PDE benchmarks, EMNGD achieves higher accuracy and faster convergence than the compared state-of-the-art baselines. Woodbury preserves the EMNGD direction, while scalable-solver diagnostics quantify the accuracy--cost trade-off of preconditioning and residual subsampling.

</details>


### [42] [Cross-Domain Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning for Contextual Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22012)
*Yuta Natsubori,Masataka Ushiku,Yuta Saito*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出跨域离策略评估与学习（Cross-Domain OPE/L）框架，通过引入源域数据来克服传统OPE/L在少样本、确定性日志策略和新动作等挑战下的方差过大与探索不足问题，并开发了相应的估计器和策略梯度方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPE/L方法在少样本数据、确定性日志策略和新动作等实际常见场景中表现不佳，主要受限于方差问题和日志数据中探索不足的局限性。

Method: 提出跨域OPE/L问题设定，综合利用目标域和源域的日志数据，开发了新的估计器和策略梯度方法。

Result: 经验评估表明，所提方法在先前难以解决的场景中显著提升了OPE/L的性能。

Conclusion: 跨域OPE/L框架有效拓展了OPE/L在现实挑战场景中的适用性，利用源域数据可以显著增强目标域的评估与学习效果。

Abstract: Off-Policy Evaluation and Learning (OPE/L) in contextual bandits is rapidly gaining popularity in real systems because new policies can be evaluated and learned securely using only historical logged data. However, existing methods in OPE/L cannot handle many challenging but prevalent scenarios such as few-shot data, deterministic logging policies, and new actions. In many applications, such as personalized medicine, content recommendations, education, and advertising, we need to evaluate and learn new policies in the presence of these challenges. Existing methods cannot evaluate and optimize effectively in these situations due to the notorious variance issue or limited exploration in the logged data. To enable OPE/L even under these unsolved challenges, we propose a new problem setup of Cross-Domain OPE/L, where we have access not only to the logged data from the target domain in which the new policy will be implemented but also to logged datasets collected from other domains. This novel formulation is widely applicable because we can often use historical data not only from the target hospital, country, device, or user segment but also from other hospitals, countries, devices, or segments. We develop a new estimator and policy gradient method to solve OPE/L by leveraging both target and source datasets, resulting in substantially enhanced OPE/L in the previously unsolved situations in our empirical evaluations.

</details>


### [43] [DCS: A Unified Conditional Sensitivity Framework for Cross-Modal Copyright Infringement Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22035)
*Xiafeng Man*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DCS（双分支条件敏感性）框架，将版权侵权检测视为反事实条件分布偏移问题，通过对比学习分支与反学习分支的模型行为差异来检测训练数据中是否包含受保护内容，并在多种模型上验证了该方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础模型可能复制或依赖受版权保护的训练内容，但单纯依靠输出相似度进行侵权检测不足，因为相似输出可能来自公共领域概念、通用风格惯例或统计泛化。

Method: 提出统一的后验检测框架，将侵权证据建模为反事实条件分布偏移。引入双分支条件敏感性（DCS），通过创建学习分支和反学习分支，利用影响函数连接二者的位移与不可观测的反事实重训练效应，并通过校准检测统计量区分目标特定记忆化与通用微调不稳定性。

Result: DCS框架适用于岭正则化线性回归、条件扩散模型、自回归语言模型和多模态模型，可通过预测间隙、图像嵌入散度、token分布/熵偏移、跨模态表征变化等不同方式实例化。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种从反事实因果角度进行版权侵权检测的通用框架，可在不依赖实际重训练的情况下识别训练数据中的受保护内容。

Abstract: Currently, most foundation models can reproduce or strongly depend on copyrighted training content, but output similarity alone is insufficient for infringement detection, because similar outputs may also arise from public-domain concepts, common stylistic conventions, or ordinary statistical generalization.
  In this paper, we develops a unified post-hoc detection framework that treats copyright infringement evidence as a counterfactual conditional distribution shift: a protected target is suspicious when the model's behavior under aligned conditions would change measurably if that target were included in, or removed from, the training process.
  We formalize this view through conditional differential privacy and introduce Dual-Branch Conditional Sensitivity (DCS), an operational statistic that measures the observable gap between two locally perturbed model states. Specifically, the proposed DCS framework creates a learning branch and an unlearning branch around the deployed model, connects their displacement to the unavailable counterfactual retraining effect through influence-function analysis, and bounds the observable sensitivity by the counterfactual privacy-budget surrogate, local curvature, training-set scale, and perturbation step size. To distinguish target-specific memorization from generic fine-tuning instability, we further define a calibrated detection statistic that subtracts the sensitivity measured under orthogonal conditions.
  The DCS framework is instantiated for ridge-regularized linear regression, conditional diffusion models, autoregressive language models, and multimodal models. These instantiations show how the same principle can be evaluated through prediction gaps, image-embedding divergence, token-distribution or entropy shifts, and cross-modal representation changes.

</details>


### [44] [CEL: Comprehensive Counterfactual Explanations Library and Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22045)
*Oleksii Furman,Łukasz Lenkiewicz,Marcel Musiałek,Maciej Zięba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CEL（反事实解释库），一个统一的库和基准测试平台，包含18个数据集和14种反事实解释方法的实现/重实现，通过标准化评估协议（涵盖有效性、覆盖率、稀疏性、邻近性和分布合理性等指标）对多种方法进行了全面定量比较，旨在提升可重复性和公平比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释研究缺乏统一的评估框架，不同研究使用不同的数据划分、预测模型和评估指标，导致方法间难以进行客观比较。

Method: 构建CEL库，统一实现14种反事实解释方法，在18个不同规模和复杂度的数据集上进行标准化评估，采用多维度互补指标（有效性、覆盖率、稀疏性、邻近性、密度和离群值为基础的分布合理性度量）。

Result: 首次在统一可重复框架下对近年反事实解释方法进行系统性基准评估，提供了全面的定量比较结果。

Conclusion: 该基准测试旨在提高可重复性、实现公平比较，并为未来反事实解释方法的发展建立工作台。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations are a prominent approach in explainable artificial intelligence (xAI), providing actionable guidance on what input changes would alter a model's prediction to a desired outcome. While early methods primarily focused on minimal feature changes, recent work incorporates additional properties such as sparsity, actionability and plausibility. Despite this progress, fair and systematic evaluation remains challenging. Existing studies often rely on different data splits, predictive models, and evaluation metrics, which limits objective comparison across methods. To fill this gap, we introduce CEL (Counterfactual Explanations Library), a unified library and benchmark for counterfactual explanations designed to support consistent implementation and evaluation. CEL includes 18 datasets of varying size and complexity and provides implementations or reimplementations of 14 widely used counterfactual methods. Using this standardized setup, we conduct a comprehensive quantitative comparison across a variety of methods on datasets that differ in size, number, and types of attributes. The evaluation protocol incorporates multiple complementary metrics capturing validity, coverage, sparsity, proximity, and distributional plausibility, including density- and outlier-based measures to assess the realism of generated counterfactuals. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first comprehensive benchmark that systematically evaluates recent counterfactual explanation methods within a unified and reproducible framework. While prior libraries and benchmarking efforts exist in the literature, many are outdated, limited in scope, or lack consistent evaluation protocols. The proposed benchmark aims to improve reproducibility, enable fair comparison, and establish a workbench for the development of future counterfactual explanation methods.

</details>


### [45] [A Leakage-Free Stacked Ensemble Method for Multiclass Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22081)
*S. P. Sharmila,Aruna Tiwari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无泄漏的堆叠集成框架LFS-FRAME，结合KAN的函数学习和XGBoost的规则学习，通过严格的out-of-fold堆叠策略防止数据泄漏，在多元分类任务上取得了89.85%（主类）和81.74%（子类）的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元分类面临类间相似度高、类别不平衡和数据分布多变等挑战。基于规则的分类器（如XGBoost）在结构化特征上表现强但难以捕获平滑函数关系，而神经网络能建模复杂非线性交互但易过拟合和泛化不足。现有方法无法有效融合两者的互补优势。

Method: 提出LFS-FRAME框架，采用两层堆叠集成策略：第一层基学习器包括KAN（用于函数学习）和XGBoost（用于规则学习），生成概率输出作为元特征；采用严格的out-of-fold堆叠策略确保训练与验证数据完全隔离，防止性能泄漏；第二层元分类器基于无偏的元特征学习，融合全局函数模式和尖锐决策边界。

Result: 在多元分类数据集上，LFS-FRAME相比强单模型基线，主类别识别准确率达89.85%，子类别识别准确率达81.74%，各项性能指标均有提升。

Conclusion: 无泄漏的函数学习与规则学习堆叠框架LFS-FRAME能有效实现可靠且泛化的多元分类。

Abstract: Multiclass classification is a fundamental problem across a wide range of domains. It is still challenging due to possession of high inter-class similarity, class imbalance datasets, and variability in data distributions. Rule-based classifiers such as XGBoost often achieve stronger performance on structured features, but they are limited in capturing smooth functional relationships among variables. Similarly, neural network models can represent complex nonlinear interactions but frequently suffer from overfitting and generalization issues. To address these limitations, we propose LFS-FRAME, a Leakage-Free Stacked ensemble framework that integrates functional learning using Kolmogorov-Arnold Networks (KAN) and rule-based learning via XGBoost for robust multiclass classification.
  The proposed framework constructs unbiased meta-features by employing a strict out-of-fold stacking strategy to ensure complete isolation between training and validation data hence preventing performance leakage. By learning over probabilistic outputs from heterogeneous base learners, the meta-classifier effectively exploits both global functional patterns and sharp decision boundaries present in the complex data. Experimental evaluations on multi-class datasets demonstrate that LFS-FRAME improves performance metrics, and overall accuracy is 89.85% in identifying major families and 81.74% in identifying sub-families relative to strong single-model baselines. These results highlight the effectiveness of leakage-free functional and rule-based stacking for reliable and generalizable multiclass classification.

</details>


### [46] [Pretraining EHR Foundation Models with Patient-Aware Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22114)
*Joshua Placidi,Yuxuan Liu,Jinpei Han,Marek Rei,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对自回归电子健康档案（EHR）基础模型中的预训练序列构建方法进行研究，发现传统全局流（Global Stream）方法存在多患者混合、长记录患者优化更新过多导致偏差的问题，提出了一种名为患者采样（Patient Sampling）的序列构建方法，通过可控加权分布训练信号，在MIMIC-IV数据集上显著提升了Macro AUROC和AUPRC指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归EHR基础模型直接沿用语言建模的预训练方法（全局流），将患者轨迹拼接为单一token流后采样窗口。然而在EHR数据中，这种方法会导致窗口可能混合多个患者，且记录较长的患者获得更多优化更新，引入了潜在偏差。因此需要提出一种更合理的序列构建方法来控制训练信号在患者间的分布。

Method: 提出患者采样（Patient Sampling）方法，这是一种预训练序列构建方法，能够控制训练信号在患者之间的分配方式。与传统的全局流（Global Stream）方法对比，患者采样采用随机采样并配合可控加权机制，使训练信号的分布更具可控性。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV v2.2和v3.1真实世界EHR数据上，患者采样方法在下游临床任务中显著优于全局流基线，提升了Macro AUROC和AUPRC指标。

Conclusion: 训练和验证序列构建是自回归EHR基础模型中重要但未被充分探索的设计选择，患者采样方法通过优化训练信号的分布有效提升了模型性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive foundation models for electronic health records (EHRs) typically inherit pretraining methods from language modeling, where patient trajectories are concatenated into a single token stream and windows are sampled from that stream. In EHR data, this choice is consequential: windows may mix multiple patients, and patients with longer records contribute more optimization updates, potentially introducing bias. We propose Patient Sampling, a pretraining sequence-construction method that allows us to control how training signal is distributed across patients. We compare this method to the standard approach, which we refer to as Global Stream. We show that stochastic Patient Sampling with controllable weighting improves performance on real-world EHR data. Across downstream clinical tasks on MIMIC-IV v2.2 and v3.1, Patient Sampling improves Macro AUROC and AUPRC over the Global Stream baseline. These results identify training and validation sequence construction as important and underexplored design choices for autoregressive EHR foundation models.

</details>


### [47] [TriGlue: a Biology-Inspired Generative Model for Generating Molecular Glue-Induced Ternary Complex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22143)
*Yuliang Yan,Shuo Yan,Haochun Tang,Yiqin Sun,Enyan Dai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为TriGlue的生物启发式生成框架，将分子胶设计形式化为三元复合物生成问题，通过界面估计和界面条件复合物生成两个耦合阶段，实现分子胶的生成与三元复合物的组装。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子胶降解剂通过诱导E3泛素连接酶与靶蛋白形成三元复合物实现靶向蛋白降解，具有巨大治疗潜力，但计算设计尚未得到充分探索，因为其受未知蛋白-蛋白界面控制，需要同时建模配体生成、蛋白-蛋白对接和三元复合物组装。

Method: 将分子胶设计形式化为三元复合物生成问题，提出TriGlue框架，包含两个耦合阶段：(1) SE(3)-等变界面估计模块，从未结合单体结构预测几何约束的蛋白-蛋白界面；(2) 界面条件三元流匹配网络，联合生成分子胶并预测组装三元复合物所需的刚体变换。

Result: 大量实验表明TriGlue能够生成化学上有效的分子，并产生合理的三元复合物结构。

Conclusion: 生物启发式生成建模有潜力加速分子胶发现。

Abstract: Molecular glue degraders have emerged as a promising strategy for targeted protein degradation by inducing ternary complex formation between an E3 ubiquitin ligase and a target protein. Despite their therapeutic potential, computational design of molecular glues remains largely unexplored. Unlike conventional structure-based drug design, molecular glue design is governed by the unknown protein-protein interface and requires the simultaneous modeling of ligand generation, protein-protein docking, and ternary complex assembly. In this work, we formulate molecular glue design as a ternary complex generation problem and propose a biology-inspired generative framework, TriGlue. Motivated by the mechanism of molecular glue action, we decompose ternary complex generation into two coupled stages: interface estimation and interface-conditioned complex generation. First, we develop an SE(3)-equivariant interface estimation module that predicts a geometrically constrained protein-protein interface from unbound monomer structures. Second, we introduce an interface-conditioned ternary flow matching network that jointly generates the molecular glue and predicts the rigid-body transformation required to assemble the ternary complex. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TriGlue generates chemically valid molecules and produces plausible ternary complexes, which highlight the potential of biology-inspired generative modeling for accelerating molecular glue discovery. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/molecular-glue-design-806B.

</details>

*L{é}o Nicollier,Marc Pic,Pablo Mus{é},Enric Meinhardt-Llopis,Gabriele Facciolo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无偏开放世界正则化（UOWReg），通过将全局正则化目标转为条件分布匹配，实现表示与目标属性的统计独立，有效缓解自监督学习中的偏差纠缠问题，在CelebA上降低均等几率违规的同时保持分类精度，并在新提出的合成雕刻任务中防止子群体坍缩。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SSL和JEPA模型虽采用全局正则化（如多元高斯或球面均匀分布）防止表示坍缩，但无法阻止任务无关特征将潜空间分割为不同子区域，导致偏差纠缠。最近方法如EnD和FSCL虽能经验性去偏，但仅是对条件分布匹配的部分近似。

Method: 提出UOWReg框架，将全局正则化转变为显式的条件分布匹配目标，确保学习表示与目标属性统计独立。支持高斯和球面两种潜空间，通过统计度量强制执行条件分布。在球面上施加条件均匀性分布。

Result: UOWReg在CelebA基准上降低了均等几率违规，同时保持与现有编码器-only基线相当的分类精度。条件球面均匀分布相比条件高斯分布获得更低的线性探测分类误差。在合成雕刻任务中有效防止了标准SSL中的子群体坍缩。

Conclusion: 从全局约束转向条件目标能保证表示与目标属性的统计独立性，且条件球面均匀分布在去偏和分类精度之间取得更优权衡。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, self-supervised learning (SSL) models and Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) remain susceptible to learning spurious biases in the dataset. These techniques rely on regularization, which prevents representation collapse by enforcing a global target distribution such as a multivariate Gaussian or a uniform distribution on the sphere. However, these global constraints are insufficient to prevent bias entanglement, as task-irrelevant features can still segregate the latent space into distinct sub-regions. While recent approaches like Entangling and Disentangling (EnD) and Fair Supervised Contrastive Learning (FSCL) empirically debias the latent space, we show that they act as partial approximations of conditional distribution matching. To enforce this matching explicitly, we propose Unbiased Open World Regularization (UOWReg), an encoder-only framework. We show that this shift from a global to a conditional objective guarantees statistical independence between the learned representations and the targeted attributes, regardless of the chosen target distribution. We empirically validate this framework across both Gaussian and spherical latent spaces, using statistical measures to enforce these target distributions. While conditional matching successfully mitigates bias with both distributions, we demonstrate that enforcing conditional uniformity on the sphere yields a lower linearprobing classification error. Empirically, UOWReg reduces Equalized Odds violations on the CelebA benchmark while maintaining competitive classification accuracy compared to existing encoder-only baselines. Furthermore, we introduce the Synthetic Engraving Task-a novel setting in which a dominant macro-structure masks a fine-grained micro-signature. We show that UOWReg effectively prevents the subpopulation collapse observed in standard SSL, successfully isolating micro-signatures even when heavily entangled with the global structure.

</details>


### [49] [From Score Approximation to Distribution Approximation in Score-Based Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22199)
*Lan V. Truong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立了分数匹配扩散模型中神经网络对分数函数的逼近误差与反向扩散过程生成的概率分布逼近目标数据分布之间的定量联系，证明了在一定条件下，分数逼近误差可以转化为KL散度意义下的分布逼近误差，并给出了显式上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于分数的扩散模型在生成建模中取得了显著的实证成功，但其逼近理论基础仍不完整。经典通用逼近定理确保神经网络可以逼近分数函数，但尚不清楚这种逼近保证能否转化为反向扩散过程生成概率分布的逼近保证。

Method: 结合Hornik通用逼近定理、路径空间上的Girsanov定理以及相对熵的数据处理不等式，推导出分布逼近误差关于分数逼近误差、扩散噪声调度和终端先验失配的显式上界。

Result: 证明了如果神经网络足够准确地逼近真实分数函数，则反向扩散模型生成的概率分布与目标数据分布之间的KL散度是有界的，该上界由分数逼近误差、扩散噪声调度和终端先验失配共同决定。

Conclusion: 本文建立了神经网络分数逼近与扩散模型分布逼近之间的严格定量桥梁，为基于经典神经网络逼近理论的分数匹配扩散模型提供了逼近理论基础，补充了现有基于有限样本统计设定和数据分布结构假设的工作。

Abstract: Score-based diffusion models have achieved remarkable empirical success in generative modeling, yet their approximation-theoretic foundations remain incomplete. In particular, although classical universal approximation theorems guarantee that neural networks can approximate score functions, it remains unclear whether such approximation guarantees translate into approximation of the probability distributions generated by reverse diffusion processes. In this paper, we establish a rigorous quantitative connection between these two notions. Specifically, we prove that if a neural network approximates the true score function sufficiently accurately, then the probability distribution generated by the corresponding reverse diffusion model is close to the target data distribution in Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence, up to an irreducible mismatch between the terminal distribution of the forward diffusion process and the prior used to initialize the reverse process. More precisely, we derive an explicit upper bound on the distribution approximation error in terms of the score approximation error, the diffusion noise schedule, and the terminal prior mismatch. Our analysis combines Hornik's universal approximation theorem, Girsanov's theorem on path space, and the data processing inequality for relative entropy. Complementary to recent work that studies score approximation under finite-sample statistical settings and structural assumptions on the data distribution, our work develops an approximation-theoretic analysis based on classical neural network approximation theory. The resulting theorem provides a simple and explicit guarantee linking neural network approximation of score functions to approximation of the probability distributions generated by reverse diffusion models.

</details>


### [50] [Latent PDE mapping for efficient physics-informed learning across geometries with limited data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22215)
*Ingvild Askim Adde,Mary M. Maleckar,Gabriel Balaban*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在PDE映射（latent PDE mapping），一种物理信息学习技术，通过变形梯度将几何特定的PDE残差和边界条件映射到预定义的潜在几何上，实现几何泛化。在心脏电生理学的Aliev-Panfilov PDE上验证，仅用15个几何样本训练，在特定几何族上实现了约4-6倍的相对L2误差降低，且计算成本适中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有物理信息机器学习方法缺乏几何一致的形状梯度，导致在面对不同几何形状时泛化能力不足，且需要大量训练数据。

Method: 提出潜在PDE映射，利用变形梯度将几何特定的PDE残差和边界条件拉回至预定义的潜在几何空间，自动计算几何一致的形状梯度。结合物理信息神经网络（PINNs）和物理信息深度算子网络（PI-DeepONets）进行求解。

Result: 在仿射和剪切变形几何上改进有限，但在特定几何族上实现约4-6倍的相对L2误差降低。训练阶段计算成本适中，推理阶段计算成本可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 潜在PDE映射能够从有限的训练几何样本中创建可泛化的物理信息机器学习模型，为几何泛化提供了一条有效路径。

Abstract: In this study, we introduce latent PDE mapping, a broadly applicable physics-informed learning technique designed to enable efficient geometric generalization with sparse training data. Latent PDE mapping pulls back geometry-specific PDE residuals and boundary conditions to a predefined latent geometry via the deformation gradient, thereby enabling the automated calculation of geometry-consistent shape gradients that are missing in conventional physics-informed machine learning formulations. We demonstrate the utility of latent PDE mapping in solving the anisotropic Aliev-Panfilov PDE of cardiac electrophysiology using both physics-informed neural networks and physics-informed deep operator networks. The Aliev-Panfilov PDE serves as a challenging exemplar: a nonlinear, time-dependent PDE benchmark with sharp gradients that are expensive to capture using traditional numerical solvers. To represent the limited data regime, we train the networks using just fifteen geometric samples drawn from parameterized distributions in two and three spatial dimensions. While modest improvements appear for geometries parameterized by affine and shear deformations, latent PDE mapping demonstrates significant benefits on select geometric families, achieving a factor ~4-6 reduction in mean relative L2 error. Furthermore, our results show that the computational cost of applying latent PDE mapping was modest during network training, and negligible at inference. Taken together, our study highlights how latent PDE mapping facilitates the creation of generalizable physics-informed machine learning models from limited sets of training geometries.

</details>


### [51] [Optimization of time-consuming experimental conditions using pseudo-experimental data guided by adaptive polynomial regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22238)
*Hirotaka Sugawara,Yujin Taguchi,Kei Minagawa,Yusuke Hiki,Takashi Morikura,Akira Funahashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolyBO方法，通过自适应更新的低容量多项式回归模型生成高质量伪实验数据，在有限实验数据下加速贝叶斯优化，在合成函数上中位优化时间减少42%，在真实材料优化问题中减少96%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯优化在实验科学中收敛慢，每次评估成本高且耗时长；现有方法虽使用伪实验数据加速优化，但在实验数据有限时生成的伪数据质量不足。

Method: PolyBO使用自适应更新的低容量多项式回归模型生成高质量伪实验数据，将实验数据与伪实验数据结合后更新BO代理模型，从而实现高效优化。

Result: 在合成基准函数上，PolyBO的中位优化时间减少42%；在真实材料成分优化问题中，中位优化时间相比传统方法减少96%。

Conclusion: PolyBO在每次实验耗时长、评估预算有限的场景下实现了高效优化，显著加速了优化过程。

Abstract: Bayesian optimization (BO) is an optimization method that sequentially proposes the next candidate explainable variables for optimizing target variables by balancing exploration and exploitation. BO is often used under a limited evaluation budget, such as hyperparameter tuning of deep learning. Despite its effectiveness, conventional BO may have poor convergence in practical experimental science where each evaluation is often costly and time-consuming. Recently, BO methods have been proposed that accelerate optimization by using pseudo-experimental data that simulate experimental data. However, when only a limited number of experimental data are available, the generated pseudo-experimental data may be of insufficient quality. In this study, we developed PolyBO to improve optimization time by generating high-quality pseudo-experimental data even when the number of trials is limited. PolyBO performs BO efficiently by generating pseudo-experimental data with an adaptively updated versatile parametric model. This low-capacity polynomial regression model is intended to enable efficient BO even with limited experimental data. PolyBO updates the BO surrogate model with a combined dataset consisting of experimental data and pseudo-experimental data and then performs optimization. Using synthetic benchmark functions with diverse landscapes, we found that PolyBO reduced the optimization time by a median of 42\%. For a real-world material composition optimization problem, PolyBO reduced the optimization time by a median of 96\% compared with conventional methods. Overall, PolyBO achieves efficient optimization in settings where each experiment requires a long time.

</details>


### [52] [IFCLoRA: Topology-Aware Rank Allocation for Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22251)
*Wei Zhang,Xinwu Liu,Yihang Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IFCLoRA提出了一种基于信息流中心性的拓扑感知秩分配方法，在微调前利用少量校准数据和冻结预训练模型，通过构建任务条件交互图并计算信息流中心性分数，为每个Transformer模块分配自适应秩，在保持标准LoRA训练成本的同时，在多个模型和任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应秩方法依赖训练过程中的局部梯度统计，这不仅引入额外内存和计算开销，还忽视了任务条件下的全局信息流结构，导致秩分配不够高效。

Method: IFCLoRA在微调前执行：1）使用小校准集和冻结预训练模型构建稀疏的任务条件交互图，节点为LoRA兼容模块；2）结合全局信息流拓扑先验与局部梯度敏感性，计算信息流中心性分数，估计各模块在多跳传播下的自适应重要性；3）在全局预算约束下一次性地分配秩。

Result: 在多个模型、任务和低秩设置下，IFCLoRA一致优于LoRA、AdaLoRA和EVA，且训练成本与标准LoRA相当。在LLaMA 3 8B数学推理任务上，秩4和秩8设置下分别提升1.36%和1.82%。

Conclusion: 全局信息流结构为低预算参数高效微调提供了信息丰富且可解释的先验，任务相关的非均匀秩分布进一步验证了该方法的有效性。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used parameter-efficient fine-tuning method for large language models, but its performance depends strongly on how a fixed rank budget is distributed across Transformer modules. Existing adaptive-rank methods usually rely on local gradient statistics collected during training, which introduces extra memory and computation and overlooks task-conditioned global information flow. We propose IFCLoRA, a topology-aware rank allocation method applied before fine-tuning. Using a small calibration set and a frozen pretrained model, IFCLoRA builds a sparse task-conditioned interaction graph whose nodes represent LoRA-compatible modules. It combines a global information-flow topology prior with local gradient sensitivity to compute Information-Flow Centrality scores, which estimate each module's adaptation importance under multi-hop propagation. Ranks are then assigned once under a global budget. Across multiple models, tasks, and low-rank settings, IFCLoRA consistently outperforms LoRA, AdaLoRA, and EVA under matched training configurations and total rank budgets, while retaining training costs comparable to standard LoRA. On mathematical reasoning with LLaMA 3 8B, IFCLoRA improves over LoRA by 1.36 percent at rank 4 and 1.82 percent at rank 8. Further analysis shows task-dependent, non-uniform rank profiles, indicating that global information-flow structure provides an informative and interpretable prior for low-budget parameter-efficient fine-tuning.

</details>


### [53] [Class-Balanced Softmax: A Bayes Theory-Based Method for Long-Tailed Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22258)
*Yi-Hang Zhu,Rajeev Raman,Shiqi Su,Jianyuan Sun,Xinyu Yang,Nan Xing,Huiyu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Class-Balanced Softmax (CBS)，一种基于贝叶斯框架和幂律假设的简单logit调整方法，用于缓解不平衡数据集中传统softmax和Balanced Softmax的性能退化问题，并通过引入偏好问题度量指标证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统softmax在不平衡数据集上性能显著下降；Balanced Softmax虽被广泛采用但存在固有限制（如尾类测试准确率不成比例地低）。

Method: 基于理论贝叶斯框架和启发式幂律假设，提出CBS作为简单的logit调整方法，计算成本低且易于集成到现有流程中；同时定义了偏好问题（preference issue），引入新指标量化该问题。

Result: 在大规模基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，CBS具有高度可扩展性，性能优于包括Balanced Softmax在内的现有方法。

Conclusion: CBS通过简单有效的logit调整，既能缓解传统softmax在不平衡数据上的性能退化，又能克服Balanced Softmax的局限性，同时有效缓解偏好问题。

Abstract: Deep learning models using traditional softmax classifiers have achieved remarkable success in various classification tasks. However, their performance degrades significantly on imbalanced datasets. Although Balanced Softmax is widely adopted as a state-of-the-art rebalancing method, it possesses inherent limitations, such as yielding disproportionately lower testing accuracy for tail classes. To mitigate these shortcomings, we propose the Class-Balanced Softmax (CBS). Rooted in a theoretical Bayesian framework and a heuristic power-law assumption, the CBS is a simple logit adjustment that is computationally inexpensive and easily integrated into existing pipelines. Furthermore, we characterise a fundamental phenomenon in models trained on imbalanced data, termed the preference issue, wherein models exhibit higher training error and a larger generalisation gap for classes with limited data. To quantify this issue, we introduce a novel metric and demonstrate that CBS effectively mitigates the preference issue. Extensive experiments on large-scale benchmarks show that CBS is highly scalable and outperforms existing methods, including Balanced Softmax.

</details>


### [54] [Autoregressive EHR Foundation Models with Multimodal Inputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22264)
*Yuxuan Liu,Joshua Placidi,Jinpei Han,Alfred John Balston,Marek Rei,A. Aldo Faisal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将辅助临床模态（如ECG波形、胸部X光图像和临床笔记）融入基于自回归基础模型的电子健康记录（EHR）预测中。通过模态特定的潜在压缩和门控交叉注意力与时间对齐，研究了两个关键设计选择：(1) 长序列的压缩方式；(2) 预训练编码器的选择。实验表明，最佳潜在压缩配置优于未压缩的交叉注意力和平均池化，更强的预训练编码器表现更好，但仅添加辅助模态并不能保证性能提升，需谨慎设计融合架构和临床评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自回归基础模型大多仅基于结构化事件编码（如EHR代码）进行训练，未能以系统化的方式融合多种临床模态（如波形、影像、文本），限制了其在零样本临床预测中的表现力。

Method: 提出一个多模态融合框架，使用模态特定的潜在压缩（latent compression）将长序列压缩为紧凑表示，再通过门控交叉注意力（gated cross-attention）与时间对齐机制，将压缩后的辅助模态特征注入到自回归EHR基础模型中。

Result: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上的消融实验表明：(1) 最佳潜在压缩配置优于未压缩的交叉注意力及均值池化；(2) 更强的预训练编码器在相应模态上持续优于较弱版本；(3) 仅添加辅助模态并不保证在ICU死亡率预测上优于仅用EHR的基线。

Conclusion: 融合架构的设计和临床背景下的适当评估至关重要，不能简单堆叠多模态数据，而需要精心设计压缩与对齐机制才能有效提升预测性能。

Abstract: Autoregressive foundation models trained on tokenized electronic health records (EHRs) can support zero-shot clinical prediction, yet most operate on structured event codes alone, and do not incorporate multiple modalities in a principled way. We present a framework for conditioning such models on auxiliary clinical modalities, including ECG waveforms, chest X-ray images, and clinical notes, using modality-specific latent compression and gated cross-attention with temporal alignment. We investigate two key design choices: (1) how to compress long per-modality sequences (e.g., ECG time series) before they enter the multi-modal cross-attention. This feature may be essential to reduce compute overheads and may be beneficial for generalization; (2) how the choice of pretrained encoder for each modality impacts downstream performance. Through controlled ablations on MIMIC-IV, we show that the best latent-compression configurations outperforms both uncompressed cross-attention and mean pooling. Encoder choice has a clear within-modality effect, with stronger pretrained encoders consistently outperforming weaker alternatives. We further show that merely adding auxiliary modalities does not guarantee improvement on ICU mortality prediction over an EHR-only baseline. This implies that careful design of the fusion architecture and an appropriate evaluation in the clinical context are required.

</details>


### [55] [An Insight on Evaluation Metrics Under the Imbalanced Case of Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22286)
*Romain Hermary,Nesryne Mejri,Djamila Aouada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了异常检测中四种常用评估指标（AUROC、AUPR、F1-score、MCC）在不同类别不平衡程度下的行为特性，通过指标景观可视化揭示指标偏好与稳定性，为跨不同不平衡比数据集的结果解释和比较提供实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测本质上面临严重的类别不平衡问题，导致评估指标的解读变得困难。尽管AUROC、AUPR、F1-score和MCC被广泛使用，但这些指标值在不平衡比变化时传达的含义不同，缺乏系统的理解和实用的解读指南。

Method: 通过分析四种异常检测指标在不同不平衡水平下的行为，聚焦于指标景观（metric landscapes）的可视化研究——将指标值与真阳性率（TPR）和真阴性率（TNR）关联起来的可视化方法，直观展示各指标的偏好和稳定性特征。

Result: 揭示了四种指标在不同不平衡比下的行为差异和稳定性特征，为理解和比较不同不平衡率数据集上的异常检测结果提供了系统性的分析结果。

Conclusion: 该研究为异常检测社区提供了实用的指标解读和比较指导，帮助研究人员和实践者更好地理解不同不平衡程度下各类评估指标的实际含义。

Abstract: Anomaly detection is inherently characterised by severe class imbalance, making the interpretation of evaluation metrics challenging. Although metrics such as AUROC, AUPR, F1-score, and MCC are widely used, their values convey different meanings depending on the anomaly ratio. In this work, we analyse the behaviour of those four common anomaly detection metrics under varying levels of imbalance. We focus on the study of metric landscapes, visualisations that relate metric values to true positive and true negative rates, providing an intuitive view of metric preferences and stability. Our analysis offers practical guidance for interpreting and comparing anomaly detection results across datasets with different imbalance ratios.

</details>


### [56] [Efficient Recommendations via Graph Coarsening and Label Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22287)
*Alessandro Sbandi,Federico Siciliano,Fabrizio Silvestri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段扩散框架，通过图粗化（社区聚合）结合标签传播算法（LPA）或轻量级图神经网络（GNN），在电信领域大规模图上实现推荐。相比全图LPA基线，LPA两阶段方案NDCG@5提升24%，引入GNN后提升超50%，并在可扩展性、延迟和推荐质量间取得最优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级图推荐系统面临图规模庞大带来的可扩展性和效率挑战，现有方法难以有效平衡预测质量与计算成本，亟需一种能系统性缩减图规模同时保持结构代表性的自适应技术。

Method: 提出灵活的两阶段扩散框架：第一阶段利用领域启发式规则将节点聚合为有意义的社区（图粗化），然后在粗化图上通过LPA或GNN进行初始扩散得到粗粒度预测；第二阶段在子图上再次执行LPA，生成面向个体用户的最终推荐。

Result: 在真实电信数据集上，两阶段均使用LPA的方案相比全图LPA基线，NDCG@5提升24%；第一阶段引入轻量级GNN后NDCG@5提升超过50%，但训练和推理时间显著增加。

Conclusion: 图粗化驱动的两阶段扩散框架能有效平衡可扩展性、延迟与推荐质量，在工业级大规模图推荐场景中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Graph-based recommendations are widely adopted in real-world industrial applications. However, graphs in these systems often reach a massive scale, posing notable scalability and efficiency challenges. This requires techniques that can effectively balance predictive quality with computational cost. One promising approach is graph coarsening, an adaptive graph reduction technique that offers a way to systematically construct smaller, yet structurally representative, versions of the original large-scale graphs.
  In this work, we propose a flexible two-stage diffusion framework that combines graph coarsening with multi-step label propagation in the telecommunications domain. Domain-specific heuristics are applied to first aggregate nodes into meaningful communities, reducing graph size while preserving essential business-relevant relationships. An initial diffusion process done by a Label Propagation Algorithm (LPA) or a Graph Neural Network (GNN) propagates labels across the coarsened graph to produce coarse-grained predictions. Finally, a second LPA within subgraphs generates the final recommendations for individual users.
  On a real-world telecommunications dataset, when using LPA in both stages, our method achieves up to +24% NDCG@5 over the full-graph LPA baseline. Incorporating a lightweight GNN in the first stage further boosts NDCG@5 by more than 50%, but requires substantial training and inference time. Through extensive experiments and a detailed ablation, we quantify these trade-offs and demonstrate that our coarsening-driven approach delivers an optimal balance between scalability, latency, and recommendation quality.

</details>
